HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000337/B003","root_000473/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0368e2fb0ed3aacae446","text":"`104:6`-`104:9` answered the question with verbs and states rather than a synonym: kindling, inward arrival, sealing, and extension. That sequence made `د ر ي B001` causative in a stronger sense, because the packet causes knowledge by walking through the mechanism. The later fiery specification did not erase the separate `ح ط م B003` scarcity-reading; it left it as a parallel activation anchored in the repeated fixed noun and the earlier reserve-building sequence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_05b842d7db2ea5207a43","text":"2. **Exploratory direction into the mechanism.** Fixed `أَدْرَىٰكَ` can activate `د ر ي B002`, aiming at or setting upon something, when later `ط ل ع B006` supplies an ascent or access-point and `ف ء د B003` supplies a heart reached or struck. The packet supplies the interrogative turn and the later inward route; I infer that causing knowledge also directs attention along that route, from name to interior target. On this exploratory reading, the question does not leave the reader outside an unknown object: it aims perception into the Crusher's access-path. The weak link is that the fixed causative form directly favors the knowledge branch.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000337/B001","root_000473/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_16ea56a3590dde9f8fa9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:5:2\",\"root_000473\",[\"B004\"]],[\"104:5:4\",\"root_000337\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Adın soruda tekrarlanmasıyla Ezici'nin anlamının okuyucunun kavrayışını delen keskin bir araştırma aracına dönüştüğü ileri sürülür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B002","root_000473/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3705793b40d33972904b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:5:2\",\"root_000473\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Soru, dikkati bilinmeyen bir nesnenin dışında bırakmayıp Ezici'nin gizli iç hedefe uzanan yoluna yöneltir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4a2496dc7cf113ac8020","text":"104:7 retrospectively explained why the question needed to interrupt: the answer is not just \"fire\" but a movement of disclosure toward أفئدة. The later closure in 104:8 also made the question feel like the last opening before knowledge becomes inescapably shut over its object.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_57a0fdd8b9dca90b5a43","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:5:2\",\"root_000473\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Bu soru, kişinin kendi hesabıyla ürettiği sanıyı keserek onu kendisine bildirilen bir bilgiye geçirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000337/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5c773882225732790785","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:5:4\",\"root_000337\",[\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ezici, içindekileri yalnızca bir kez kıran değil, kapalı ve ısıtılmış bir süreçte iç merkezlerine kadar dönüştüren tüketici bir düzenek olarak belirir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000337/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8e318f57de395df05c6a","text":"3. **The repeated name sharpens the crusher.** Evidence: repeated `ح ط م B001`, with `د ر ي B004` (sharp horn/comb-like implement) as exploratory support. Structural cue: الحطمة is named in 104:4, questioned in 104:5, then explained in 104:6-7. Assigned role: repetition holds the object before definition; the sharp implement branch supplies a probing edge. Abductive move: the text makes the reader pause over the name so that \"crusher\" is felt before its medium is supplied. Reading change: الحطمة changes from an assumed noun into a problem that cuts into the reader's understanding.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9077d892bf8edeb700cb","text":"2. **The question aims at the hidden target.** Evidence: `د ر ي B002` (aiming at/setting upon) and `د ر ي B003` (stealth and concealment in hunting), activated by `ط ل ع B003/B004` and `ف ء د B002` in 104:7. Structural cue: the question delays definition until fire reaches the hearts. Assigned role: درى provides the act of approaching a concealed object. Abductive move: because the vice includes hidden لمز and settled inner حساب, true knowledge must stalk and expose what is concealed. Reading change: ما أدراك is not only an awe-formula; it becomes the opening of a targeted investigation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000337/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_bf96f9b16c891d8cafd9","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:5:4\",\"root_000337\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Ezici'nin anlam alanındaki kıtlık ve yıkıcı darlık, biriktirilmiş servetin gelecekteki ihtiyacı yenebileceği beklentisini boşa çıkaran ayrı bir olasılık sunar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000337/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_bfa69ea10f9493882242","text":"3. **The Crusher as a consuming processor.** Fixed `ح ط م B008` images a grinder or digester that consumes, and this dormant branch is activated by `و ق د B001` (kindled burning), `ف ء د B001` (heat used to roast or bake), and `ف ء د B002` (the heart as inner kindling). Sealing through `و ص د B001` supplies a closed chamber. The packet gives grinding, heat, an inward object, and enclosure; I infer their coordination as processing stages. `مَا ٱلْحُطَمَةُ` can consequently ask after an apparatus that reduces and transforms its contents all the way to their inner core, not merely a blow that shatters once.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000337/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_db477c2b528ac70af59e","text":"4. **A scarcity-reading that defeats stored wealth.** Fixed `ح ط م B003` directly includes a severe year, famine, or drought that breaks people and property. Its placement after gathering wealth (`ج م ع B001`, `م و ل B001`) and preparing a reserve (`ع د د B002`) makes that branch functional. The packet supplies accumulation followed by a named breaker; I infer scarcity as the contingency the reserve was meant to master but cannot. This reading coexists with the fiery one supplied next: `ٱلْحُطَمَةُ` can expose the hoarder's fantasy through an economic-ecological mechanism of depletion as well as through material crushing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000473/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e0f037fa1c75060e8df5","text":"1. **Knowledge delivered as an operational sequence.** Fixed `أَدْرَىٰكَ` realizes `د ر ي B001`, knowing and causing another to know. The packet supplies that ordered answer; I infer that each later clause defines a stage rather than merely adding attributes.
